I removed facade.mjs and adapter.mjs after reading all supplied invoice sources and their imports. Both forwarding layers had one supplied caller and made no decisions. Calculator still owns numeric calculation; formatter and exporter contracts are unchanged.\\n\\nThe returned total is rows.reduce((total,row) => total + row.qty * row.unitPrice, 0). Changing a row's qty or unitPrice, adding or removing rows, or replacing rows changes the next calculation. Mutating rows alone does not change an already returned controller result or the cached number. Calling either calculate or invoiceController overwrites the cache. A fresh module instance starts its cache at zero. The existing result object contains a number snapshot, not a live binding. A caller can also mutate its own returned object, independently of the cache.\\n\\nMinimize Reader Load removed two trace layers. I retained cachedTotal because inspectCachedTotal exposes it, and the supplied decision record explicitly names its observable contract. The probe exercises that accessor after controller and direct calculator calls. Removing the cache would require a changed accessor contract or moving its ownership to an explicit instance; I did not silently remove the export. Thus hidden module state remains a documented limitation of this scoped refactor. No reader completion time was measured.\\n\\nBefore and after probes both returned totals 6, 9.25, 17 and 0 on the selected paths. They checked that earlier result snapshots stay unchanged, missing rows still throw, and formatter results remain 0.00, 1.00, -1.00 and 12.35. Empty formatting remains [], and NaN and Infinity still throw nonfinite. These are local fixture observations only.\\n\\n## Variants attempted and limits\\n\\nOriginal copied modules were executed before editing; migrated modules were executed after editing. [\\\\\\\"trace-load/installed-source/SKILL.md\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"trace-load/installed-source/host-capabilities.md\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"trace-load/installed-source/case.json\\\\\\\",...(await Array.fromAsync(new Bun.Glob(\\\\\\\"trace-load/before/*\\\\\\\").scan(\\\\\\\".\\\\\\\"))).sort()]) { console.log(JSON.stringify({path,content:await Bun.file(path).text()})); }\\\"\\n  ],\\n  \\\"exitCode\\\": 0,\\n  \\\"stdout\\\": \\\"{\\\\\\\"path\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"trace-load/installed-source/SKILL.md\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"---\\\\\\\\nname: principle-minimize-reader-load\\\\\\\\ndescription: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Apply when reviewing or shaping code that's hard to trace. Count layers between question and answer, and hidden state in the reader's head; collapse one-caller wrappers and shrink mutable scope.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\ndisable-model-invocation: true\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n# Minimize Reader Load\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nMaintainability is the work a reader must do to understand code. Track two axes:\\\\\\\\n1. **Layers to trace.** How many indirections sit between the question and the answer.\\\\\\\\n2. **State to hold.** How much hidden or mutable context the reader must keep in their head.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Why:** Code is read far more than it is written. LOC, cyclomatic complexity, and \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"clean architecture\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" are proxies. Reader load is the thing that matters. The two axes are independent. A flat file with 50 globals can be as hard to reason about as a 6-layer adapter stack. Guard both. This is the human analog of [Guard the Context Window](../principle-guard-the-context-window/SKILL.md). Working memory is finite for readers too.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**The pattern:**\\\\\\\\n- **Collapse layers** that cost more than they save: wrappers with one caller, adapters with no second implementation, speculative indirection that was never needed. Inline them.\\\\\\\\n- **Make adjacent layers change the abstraction.** A layer that repeats the same methods and arguments adds reader load without compression. Collapse pass-through layers.\\\\\\\\n- **Demand interface compression.** A broad interface that hides little complexity makes readers learn both the surface and the implementation. Prefer boundaries that hide meaningful decisions.\\\\\\\\n- **Shrink state scope:** prefer pure functions (returns over mutations), locals over fields, fields over module state, and module state over globals. Derive instead of sync.\\\\\\\\n- **Name the invariant at the boundary,** not in every consumer, so the reader learns it once.\\\\\\\\n- Before adding a layer or a piece of state, ask: does this reduce reader load somewhere else by at least as much?\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**The test:** Can a new reader answer \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"where does X come from?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" and \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"what can change X?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" in under 30 seconds? If not, cut layers or cut state.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## Session capabilities and permissions\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nUse [host capabilities](../../references/host-capabilities.md) to discover available read, write and execution tools. Inspect the concrete path, its callers and mutable state before deciding which layers to collapse. Make edits only within the authorized task and available write permissions. Run appropriate checks only through observed execution tools.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nWhen source is readable but writes or execution are denied or unavailable, give a source-grounded proposal and identify the edits or checks you could not perform. Without source or sufficient caller and state evidence, report the missing evidence and stop concrete refactoring claims. Do not claim a measured reader time without observation. Preserve invocation metadata without assuming the host enforces it.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"}\\\\n{\\\\\\\"path\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"trace-load/installed-source/host-capabilities.md\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"# Discover capabilities in the current session\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nRead the tools exposed to this session before assigning work. Cached official-subagents.txt is documentation context; it proves no operation succeeded. Local agent configuration is not portable plugin configuration.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nRecord each binding as `{operation, tool, exposed, succeeded, evidence, limits}`. `tool` is the exact exposed identifier or null when absent. `succeeded` is true only after observing a result. Keep failed and unavailable bindings. Successful bindings become `successfulOperations` in host evidence, with a root-relative evidence file. The operation labels below describe purposes, never callable APIs.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n| Operation | Discovery and observation required |\\\\\\\\n| --- | --- |\\\\\\\\n| spawn | Find an exposed independent-worker operation; retain returned worker identity and exact assigned revision. |\\\\\\\\n| follow-up | Find the operation that sends input to that worker; observe acceptance by the same identity. |\\\\\\\\n| wait | Find the operation that waits for worker state; retain terminal or interrupted state. |\\\\\\\\n| inspect-result | Read the worker result and its referenced artifacts; a wait notification alone is insufficient. |\\\\\\\\n| stop | Observe cancellation or termination through the exposed operation. |\\\\\\\\n| read | Observe the requested file content through the available file tool. |\\\\\\\\n| isolated-write | Observe a write within the worker's assigned path and permissions. |\\\\\\\\n| history | Observe available prior turns or records; declare gaps explicitly. |\\\\\\\\n| execution | Observe command execution with exit status and output. |\\\\\\\\n| persistence | Write and read an authorized durable record; a claimed save is insufficient. |\\\\\\\\n| external-write | Record explicit authorization and action identity; inspect the result before replay. |\\\\\\\\n| resolve-ownership | Observe distinct owned paths before contested writes. |\\\\\\\\n| inspect-terminal | Observe the worker's terminal state after stop. |\\\\\\\\n| quarantine-artifacts | Observe partial artifacts withheld from integration. |\\\\\\\\n| inspect-prior-action | Read the interrupted action identity and prior outcome. |\\\\\\\\n| inspect-destination | Observe the destination state for that action identity. |\\\\\\\\n| safe-recovery | Observe explicitly authorized idempotent recovery after identity and destination inspection. |\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nNo default API names are assigned here. Tool names differ by host and session. An exposed tool may still be denied or fail. Record requested and actual models only when observed; otherwise use `unknown`. A requested model never proves the actual model.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nA denied or unavailable operation blocks the branch that requires it. Sequential substitution for required independent work is reduced capability and uses `reduced-pass`; ordinary direct completion can fully pass. Missing persistence limits session pickup even when current work succeeds. Preserve user authorization and host permission boundaries through every fallback.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nWorker passes require observed spawn and result inspection. Each registry entry declares `requiredObservations` for `pass` and `reduced-pass`; tool observations need exposed bindings and existing evidence artifacts; native text observations use the separate reviewed native record below. Full cancellation requires stop, terminal inspection and artifact quarantine. Full persistence requires durable write and readback. Full interrupted-action recovery requires prior identity inspection, destination inspection, safe recovery, explicit external permission and a recorded prior action identity even when no external-write observation is listed.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nPermission checks follow performed operations. Isolated writes and persistence require granted writes. External writes and safe recovery require granted external permission and prior identity. Authorized read-only reductions may retain denied writes; their limits must preserve denied or unavailable permissions. Required observations remain applicable to those reductions.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nHost evidence is stored as object or array JSON files under `docs/parity/evidence/`. Required fields are defined by `docs/parity/evidence.schema.json`. Artifacts and operation evidence must be existing files inside the tested root. Test fixtures belong in `tests/` and prove validator behavior only.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nRun `python3 tools/validate_evidence.py --root . --commit <tested-package-commit>` through Bun, for example `bun -e 'const p=Bun.spawnSync([\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"python3\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"-B\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"tools/validate_evidence.py\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"--root\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\".\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"--commit\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",process.argv[1]],{env:{...process.env,PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE:\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"1\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"}});process.stdout.write(p.stdout);process.stderr.write(p.stderr);process.exit(p.exitCode)' <tested-package-commit>`. It reads every JSON record below the evidence directory, prints each error, and exits 1 for invalid records or 0 for valid records. An empty directory has no invalid records and supplies no behavioral evidence.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nThe required registry file in `docs/parity/case-registry.json` declares applicability. Missing or non-file registry, unknown case, structural-only entry or unlisted host rejects behavioral validation. Maintenance fixtures use a temporary copy of this registry; they cannot bypass applicability. Required hosts must receive observed evidence or explicit blocked records before release claims. Structural checks have no required host and cannot populate host status. Later producers propose task-owned entries; only the coordinator integrates them. Source paths select callers through the task 3 case contract. Case definitions alone leave host statuses `not-run`.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## Direct completion and recorded observations\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n`direct` means the task is completed in the current context. Restatement, supplied-text writing and complete installed discovery can pass directly. `sequential` is reserved for replacing a required independent method with sequential work and remains reduced. A text response has no invented callable tool. Record supplied input, the actual response and qualitative outcome review as `nativeObservations`, each with its existing listed evidence artifact and named reviewer. Their labels are `supplied-input`, `response` and `review-outcome`. The reviewer assesses the case's expected outcome; validation checks evidence relationships and does not decide prose quality.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nEach registry entry declares allowed execution modes and required permissions for each status. A grant is required for the performed action: worker spawn, command execution and stop need execution; isolated output or persistence needs writes; external actions need their separate permission and prior identity. Read-only discovery does not inherit an execution grant requirement. Tool observations still name an actually exposed tool and existing evidence. Native observations cannot claim tool discovery, worker identity or saved writes.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nFull Arena and Swarm results additionally provide `inputRevision` and a listed `methodReceipt` artifact. An exposed `inspect-method` operation must reference that receipt. Assignment operations name `assignmentId` and the corresponding inspected output. See the [structured method contract](orchestration-contract.md). All positive maintenance fixtures are synthetic; none is an observed host result.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"}\\\\n{\\\\\\\"path\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"trace-load/installed-source/case.json\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"{\\\\\\\\n  \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"id\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"trace-load\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\n  \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"sourcePaths\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": [\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"skills/principle-minimize-reader-load/SKILL.md\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  ],\\\\\\\\n  \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"prompt\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Trace where invoice.total comes from through Controller -> InvoiceFacade -> InvoiceAdapter -> Calculator. The facade and adapter each have one caller and pass identical arguments. Calculator synchronizes a cached module total with invoice rows. Reduce indirection in this concrete path and explain what can change total.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\n  \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"requirements\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": [\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Read source and callers; writes and execution require available tools and authorization.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Inspect both layers to trace and state to hold.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Preserve meaningful abstraction boundaries and invariants.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  ],\\\\\\\\n  \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"expectedFull\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Inspect the concrete path and caller evidence. Collapse unnecessary one-caller and pass-through layers, keep boundaries that hide meaningful decisions, derive total from rows in a pure function or smaller state scope, and name the invariant once at the boundary. Explain where total comes from and what can change it. Verify authorized edits with observed checks; treat the under-30-second reader test as a criterion unless actually measured.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\n  \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"expectedReduced\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"With readable source but unavailable or denied writes or execution, give a source-grounded ordered proposal that reduces trace layers and hidden state. State which edits or checks were not performed and preserve permission limits.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\n  \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"expectedBlocked\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Without readable source or sufficient caller and state evidence, identify the missing access or evidence and stop concrete refactoring and completion claims.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\n  \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"forbiddenClaims\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": [\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"A structural validator pass proves host behavior or a measured reader time.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Fewer lines alone prove reduced reader load.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"All abstraction layers should be removed regardless of interface compression.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Unavailable writes or execution succeeded.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Preserved invocation metadata proves host enforcement.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  ]\\\\\\\\n}\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"}\\\\n{\\\\\\\"path\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"trace-load/before/adapter.mjs\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"import { calculate } from './calculator.mjs';\\\\\\\\nexport function invoiceAdapter(invoice) { return calculate(invoice.rows); }\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"}\\\\n{\\\\\\\"path\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"trace-load/before/calculator.mjs\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"let cachedTotal = 0;\\\\\\\\nexport function calculate(rows) {\\\\\\\\n  cachedTotal = rows.reduce((total, row) => total + row.qty * row.unitPrice, 0);\\\\\\\\n  return cachedTotal;\\\\\\\\n}\\\\\\\\nexport function inspectCachedTotal() { return cachedTotal; }\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"}\\\\n{\\\\\\\"path\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"trace-load/before/controller.mjs\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"import { invoiceFacade } from './facade.mjs';\\\\\\\\nexport function invoiceController(invoice) { return { total: invoiceFacade(invoice) }; }\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"}\\\\n{\\\\\\\"path\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"trace-load/before/decision.md\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"# Synthetic invoice decision record\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nDisplay contract: JavaScript Number.toFixed(2), including its binary floating-point rounding behavior. Do not silently replace this with accounting decimal rules. Empty row lists produce []. Nonfinite amounts throw Error('nonfinite'). Capture observed outputs before edits for 0, 1.005, -1.005, 12.345, empty rows, NaN and Infinity. Output examples in a test are expectations, not proof a test ran.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nFixture history decision: a UI consumer compares two-decimal strings and a CSV consumer imports formatRows. This is why rounding belongs at the display boundary. No customer statement or external ticket was retrieved. Errors are thrown by formatInvoice and are not swallowed by the controller. Alternative A removes fixed and lastFormatted; alternative B introduces a stateful Formatter class. Compare reader cost and behavioral equivalence.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nAuthorized internal rename target for preserved-behavior: formatInvoice -> formatAmount. Migrate imports, docs and string references together. sortAmounts is a known unrelated defect and must remain separate. Calculator cache is observable through inspectCachedTotal; changing it requires checking that consumer.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"}\\\\n{\\\\\\\"path\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"trace-load/before/exporter.mjs\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"import { formatRows } from './formatter.mjs';\\\\\\\\nexport function exportAmounts(rows) { return formatRows(rows).join('\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n'); }\\\\\\\\nexport const publicFixtureSymbols = ['formatInvoice', 'formatRows'];\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"}\\\\n{\\\\\\\"path\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"trace-load/before/facade.mjs\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"import { invoiceAdapter } from './adapter.mjs';\\\\\\\\nexport function invoiceFacade(invoice) { return invoiceAdapter(invoice); }\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"}\\\\n{\\\\\\\"path\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"trace-load/before/formatter.mjs\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"let lastFormatted = '';\\\\\\\\nfunction fixed(value) { return value.toFixed(2); }\\\\\\\\nexport function formatInvoice(value) {\\\\\\\\n  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) throw new Error('nonfinite');\\\\\\\\n  lastFormatted = fixed(value);\\\\\\\\n  return lastFormatted;\\\\\\\\n}\\\\\\\\nexport function formatRows(rows) { return rows.map(row => formatInvoice(row.total)); }\\\\\\\\n// Separate known bug, outside formatter refactor: this sorts numeric strings lexically.\\\\\\\\nexport function sortAmounts(values) { return values.toSorted(); }\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"}\\\\n\\\",\\n  \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\"\\n}\\n```\\n\",\n        \"artifact\": {\n          \"path\": \"fixtures/rerun-03/trace-load.result.md\",\n I removed facade.mjs and adapter.mjs after reading all supplied invoice sources and their imports. Both forwarding layers had one supplied caller and made no decisions. Calculator still owns numeric calculation; formatter and exporter contracts are unchanged.\\n\\nThe returned total is rows.reduce((total,row) => total + row.qty * row.unitPrice, 0). Changing a row's qty or unitPrice, adding or removing rows, or replacing rows changes the next calculation. Mutating rows alone does not change an already returned controller result or the cached number. Calling either calculate or invoiceController overwrites the cache. A fresh module instance starts its cache at zero. The existing result object contains a number snapshot, not a live binding. A caller can also mutate its own returned object, independently of the cache.\\n\\nMinimize Reader Load removed two trace layers. I retained cachedTotal because inspectCachedTotal exposes it, and the supplied decision record explicitly names its observable contract. The probe exercises that accessor after controller and direct calculator calls. Removing the cache would require a changed accessor contract or moving its ownership to an explicit instance; I did not silently remove the export. Thus hidden module state remains a documented limitation of this scoped refactor. No reader completion time was measured.\\n\\nBefore and after probes both returned totals 6, 9.25, 17 and 0 on the selected paths. They checked that earlier result snapshots stay unchanged, missing rows still throw, and formatter results remain 0.00, 1.00, -1.00 and 12.35. Empty formatting remains [], and NaN and Infinity still throw nonfinite. These are local fixture observations only.\\n\\n## Variants attempted and limits\\n\\nOriginal copied modules were executed before editing; migrated modules were executed after editing. \"import { readdir, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';\\nconst report = `# trace-load native execution record\\n\\n## Actual response\\n\\nThe original total comes from controller.mjs calling invoiceFacade(invoice), then invoiceAdapter(invoice), then calculate(invoice.rows). Each wrapper has one caller in the supplied invoice source. The facade forwards the invoice unchanged; the adapter selects rows. Neither hides a separate policy or implementation. The broader shared-fixture search is retained in evidence/callers.json. It also finds a separate shared/invoice.mjs fixture, which is not imported by this concrete path and was not edited.\\n\\nIn the case-owned after/ copy, the controller calls calculate(invoice.rows) directly. The facade and adapter files were removed. The controller still constructs { total }, and calculate remains the boundary for the observable calculator cache. sumRows is now a pure derivation. The one invariant comment at that derivation states that total is the left-to-right JavaScript Number sum of qty * unitPrice starting at 0. There is no rounding, decimal arithmetic replacement, finite-number validation, or coercion change here.\\n\\nChanges to qty, unitPrice, row membership, or row order can change the next result. JavaScript floating-point addition makes order relevant. A successful calculate call, including a controller call, replaces the module cache. Mutating rows alone does not refresh the cache or an already-returned total. An exception during reduction leaves the previous cache intact. NaN and Infinity propagate through calculation. Calling sumRows alone does not update the cache. No invoice.total input field is read or mutated.\\n\\nThe original path requires tracing four modules; the new path uses two modules. The extra pure function is next to calculate in calculator.mjs. The cache remains because inspectCachedTotal exposes it. Removing that state would change the supplied observable API. Readers still need to understand this one module-scoped value when using the inspection API. Fewer files are a structural observation, not a measured comprehension improvement.\\n\\nThe decision record describes a UI comparing two-decimal strings and the CSV consumer importing formatRows. Array.fromAsync(new Bun.Glob(\\\\\\\"trace-load/before/*\\\\\\\").scan(\\\\\\\".\\\\\\\"))).sort()]) { console.log(JSON.stringify({path,content:await Bun.file(path).text()})); }\\\"\\n  ],\\n  \\\"exitCode\\\": 0,\\n  \\\"stdout\\\": \\\"{\\\\\\\"path\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"trace-load/installed-source/SKILL.md\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"---\\\\\\\\nname: principle-minimize-reader-load\\\\\\\\ndescription: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Apply when reviewing or shaping code that's hard to trace. Count layers between question and answer, and hidden state in the reader's head; collapse one-caller wrappers and shrink mutable scope.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\ndisable-model-invocation: true\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n# Minimize Reader Load\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nMaintainability is the work a reader must do to understand code. Track two axes:\\\\\\\\n1. **Layers to trace.** How many indirections sit between the question and the answer.\\\\\\\\n2. **State to hold.** How much hidden or mutable context the reader must keep in their head.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Why:** Code is read far more than it is written. LOC, cyclomatic complexity, and \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"clean architecture\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" are proxies. Reader load is the thing that matters. The two axes are independent. A flat file with 50 globals can be as hard to reason about as a 6-layer adapter stack. Guard both. This is the human analog of [Guard the Context Window](../principle-guard-the-context-window/SKILL.md). Working memory is finite for readers too.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**The pattern:**\\\\\\\\n- **Collapse layers** that cost more than they save: wrappers with one caller, adapters with no second implementation, speculative indirection that was never needed. Inline them.\\\\\\\\n- **Make adjacent layers change the abstraction.** A layer that repeats the same methods and arguments adds reader load without compression. Collapse pass-through layers.\\\\\\\\n- **Demand interface compression.** A broad interface that hides little complexity makes readers learn both the surface and the implementation. Prefer boundaries that hide meaningful decisions.\\\\\\\\n- **Shrink state scope:** prefer pure functions (returns over mutations), locals over fields, fields over module state, and module state over globals. Derive instead of sync.\\\\\\\\n- **Name the invariant at the boundary,** not in every consumer, so the reader learns it once.\\\\\\\\n- Before adding a layer or a piece of state, ask: does this reduce reader load somewhere else by at least as much?\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**The test:** Can a new reader answer \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"where does X come from?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" and \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"what can change X?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" in under 30 seconds? If not, cut layers or cut state.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## Session capabilities and permissions\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nUse [host capabilities](../../references/host-capabilities.md) to discover available read, write and execution tools. Inspect the concrete path, its callers and mutable state before deciding which layers to collapse. Make edits only within the authorized task and available write permissions. Run appropriate checks only through observed execution tools.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nWhen source is readable but writes or execution are denied or unavailable, give a source-grounded proposal and identify the edits or checks you could not perform. Without source or sufficient caller and state evidence, report the missing evidence and stop concrete refactoring claims. Do not claim a measured reader time without observation. Preserve invocation metadata without assuming the host enforces it.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"}\\\\n{\\\\\\\"path\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"trace-load/installed-source/host-capabilities.md\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"# Discover capabilities in the current session\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nRead the tools exposed to this session before assigning work. Cached official-subagents.txt is documentation context; it proves no operation succeeded. Local agent configuration is not portable plugin configuration.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nRecord each binding as `{operation, tool, exposed, succeeded, evidence, limits}`. `tool` is the exact exposed identifier or null when absent. `succeeded` is true only after observing a result. Keep failed and unavailable bindings. Successful bindings become `successfulOperations` in host evidence, with a root-relative evidence file. The operation labels below describe purposes, never callable APIs.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n| Operation | Discovery and observation required |\\\\\\\\n| --- | --- |\\\\\\\\n| spawn | Find an exposed independent-worker operation; retain returned worker identity and exact assigned revision. |\\\\\\\\n| follow-up | Find the operation that sends input to that worker; observe acceptance by the same identity. |\\\\\\\\n| wait | Find the operation that waits for worker state; retain terminal or interrupted state. |\\\\\\\\n| inspect-result | Read the worker result and its referenced artifacts; a wait notification alone is insufficient. |\\\\\\\\n| stop | Observe cancellation or termination through the exposed operation. |\\\\\\\\n| read | Observe the requested file content through the available file tool. |\\\\\\\\n| isolated-write | Observe a write within the worker's assigned path and permissions. |\\\\\\\\n| history | Observe available prior turns or records; declare gaps explicitly. |\\\\\\\\n| execution | Observe command execution with exit status and output. |\\\\\\\\n| persistence | Write and read an authorized durable record; a claimed save is insufficient. |\\\\\\\\n| external-write | Record explicit authorization and action identity; inspect the result before replay. |\\\\\\\\n| resolve-ownership | Observe distinct owned paths before contested writes. |\\\\\\\\n| inspect-terminal | Observe the worker's terminal state after stop. |\\\\\\\\n| quarantine-artifacts | Observe partial artifacts withheld from integration. |\\\\\\\\n| inspect-prior-action | Read the interrupted action identity and prior outcome. |\\\\\\\\n| inspect-destination | Observe the destination state for that action identity. |\\\\\\\\n| safe-recovery | Observe explicitly authorized idempotent recovery after identity and destination inspection. |\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nNo default API names are assigned here. Tool names differ by host and session. An exposed tool may still be denied or fail. Record requested and actual models only when observed; otherwise use `unknown`. A requested model never proves the actual model.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nA denied or unavailable operation blocks the branch that requires it. Sequential substitution for required independent work is reduced capability and uses `reduced-pass`; ordinary direct completion can fully pass. Missing persistence limits session pickup even when current work succeeds. Preserve user authorization and host permission boundaries through every fallback.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nWorker passes require observed spawn and result inspection. Each registry entry declares `requiredObservations` for `pass` and `reduced-pass`; tool observations need exposed bindings and existing evidence artifacts; native text observations use the separate reviewed native record below. Full cancellation requires stop, terminal inspection and artifact quarantine. Full persistence requires durable write and readback. Full interrupted-action recovery requires prior identity inspection, destination inspection, safe recovery, explicit external permission and a recorded prior action identity even when no external-write observation is listed.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nPermission checks follow performed operations. Isolated writes and persistence require granted writes. External writes and safe recovery require granted external permission and prior identity. Authorized read-only reductions may retain denied writes; their limits must preserve denied or unavailable permissions. Required observations remain applicable to those reductions.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nHost evidence is stored as object or array JSON files under `docs/parity/evidence/`. Required fields are defined by `docs/parity/evidence.schema.json`. Artifacts and operation evidence must be existing files inside the tested root. Test fixtures belong in `tests/` and prove validator behavior only.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nRun `python3 tools/validate_evidence.py --root . --commit <tested-package-commit>` through Bun, for example `bun -e 'const p=Bun.spawnSync([\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"python3\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"-B\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"tools/validate_evidence.py\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"--root\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\".\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"--commit\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",process.argv[1]],{env:{...process.env,PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE:\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"1\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"}});process.stdout.write(p.stdout);process.stderr.write(p.stderr);process.exit(p.exitCode)' <tested-package-commit>`. It reads every JSON record below the evidence directory, prints each error, and exits 1 for invalid records or 0 for valid records. An empty directory has no invalid records and supplies no behavioral evidence.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nThe required registry file in `docs/parity/case-registry.json` declares applicability. Missing or non-file registry, unknown case, structural-only entry or unlisted host rejects behavioral validation. Maintenance fixtures use a temporary copy of this registry; they cannot bypass applicability. Required hosts must receive observed evidence or explicit blocked records before release claims. Structural checks have no required host and cannot populate host status. Later producers propose task-owned entries; only the coordinator integrates them. Source paths select callers through the task 3 case contract. Case definitions alone leave host statuses `not-run`.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## Direct completion and recorded observations\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n`direct` means the task is completed in the current context. Restatement, supplied-text writing and complete installed discovery can pass directly. `sequential` is reserved for replacing a required independent method with sequential work and remains reduced. A text response has no invented callable tool. Record supplied input, the actual response and qualitative outcome review as `nativeObservations`, each with its existing listed evidence artifact and named reviewer. Their labels are `supplied-input`, `response` and `review-outcome`. The reviewer assesses the case's expected outcome; validation checks evidence relationships and does not decide prose quality.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nEach registry entry declares allowed execution modes and required permissions for each status. A grant is required for the performed action: worker spawn, command execution and stop need execution; isolated output or persistence needs writes; external actions need their separate permission and prior identity. Read-only discovery does not inherit an execution grant requirement. Tool observations still name an actually exposed tool and existing evidence. Native observations cannot claim tool discovery, worker identity or saved writes.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nFull Arena and Swarm results additionally provide `inputRevision` and a listed `methodReceipt` artifact. An exposed `inspect-method` operation must reference that receipt. Assignment operations name `assignmentId` and the corresponding inspected output. See the [structured method contract](orchestration-contract.md). All positive maintenance fixtures are synthetic; none is an observed host result.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"}\\\\n{\\\\\\\"path\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"trace-load/installed-source/case.json\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"{\\\\\\\\n  \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"id\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"trace-load\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\n  \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"sourcePaths\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": [\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"skills/principle-minimize-reader-load/SKILL.md\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  ],\\\\\\\\n  \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"prompt\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Trace where invoice.total comes from through Controller -> InvoiceFacade -> InvoiceAdapter -> Calculator. The facade and adapter each have one caller and pass identical arguments. Calculator synchronizes a cached module total with invoice rows. Reduce indirection in this concrete path and explain what can change total.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\n  \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"requirements\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": [\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Read source and callers; writes and execution require available tools and authorization.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Inspect both layers to trace and state to hold.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Preserve meaningful abstraction boundaries and invariants.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  ],\\\\\\\\n  \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"expectedFull\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Inspect the concrete path and caller evidence. Collapse unnecessary one-caller and pass-through layers, keep boundaries that hide meaningful decisions, derive total from rows in a pure function or smaller state scope, and name the invariant once at the boundary. Explain where total comes from and what can change it. Verify authorized edits with observed checks; treat the under-30-second reader test as a criterion unless actually measured.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\n  \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"expectedReduced\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"With readable source but unavailable or denied writes or execution, give a source-grounded ordered proposal that reduces trace layers and hidden state. State which edits or checks were not performed and preserve permission limits.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\n  \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"expectedBlocked\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Without readable source or sufficient caller and state evidence, identify the missing access or evidence and stop concrete refactoring and completion claims.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\n  \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"forbiddenClaims\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": [\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"A structural validator pass proves host behavior or a measured reader time.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Fewer lines alone prove reduced reader load.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"All abstraction layers should be removed regardless of interface compression.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Unavailable writes or execution succeeded.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Preserved invocation metadata proves host enforcement.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  ]\\\\\\\\n}\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"}\\\\n{\\\\\\\"path\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"trace-load/before/adapter.mjs\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"import { calculate } from './calculator.mjs';\\\\\\\\nexport function invoiceAdapter(invoice) { return calculate(invoice.rows); }\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"}\\\\n{\\\\\\\"path\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"trace-load/before/calculator.mjs\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"let cachedTotal = 0;\\\\\\\\nexport function calculate(rows) {\\\\\\\\n  cachedTotal = rows.reduce((total, row) => total + row.qty * row.unitPrice, 0);\\\\\\\\n  return cachedTotal;\\\\\\\\n}\\\\\\\\nexport function inspectCachedTotal() { return cachedTotal; }\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"}\\\\n{\\\\\\\"path\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"trace-load/before/controller.mjs\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"import { invoiceFacade } from './facade.mjs';\\\\\\\\nexport function invoiceController(invoice) { return { total: invoiceFacade(invoice) }; }\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"}\\\\n{\\\\\\\"path\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"trace-load/before/decision.md\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"# Synthetic invoice decision record\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nDisplay contract: JavaScript Number.toFixed(2), including its binary floating-point rounding behavior. Do not silently replace this with accounting decimal rules. Empty row lists produce []. Nonfinite amounts throw Error('nonfinite'). Capture observed outputs before edits for 0, 1.005, -1.005, 12.345, empty rows, NaN and Infinity. Output examples in a test are expectations, not proof a test ran.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nFixture history decision: a UI consumer compares two-decimal strings and a CSV consumer imports formatRows. This is why rounding belongs at the display boundary. No customer statement or external ticket was retrieved. Errors are thrown by formatInvoice and are not swallowed by the controller. Alternative A removes fixed and lastFormatted; alternative B introduces a stateful Formatter class. Compare reader cost and behavioral equivalence.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nAuthorized internal rename target for preserved-behavior: formatInvoice -> formatAmount. Migrate imports, docs and string references together. sortAmounts is a known unrelated defect and must remain separate. Calculator cache is observable through inspectCachedTotal; changing it requires checking that consumer.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"}\\\\n{\\\\\\\"path\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"trace-load/before/exporter.mjs\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"import { formatRows } from './formatter.mjs';\\\\\\\\nexport function exportAmounts(rows) { return formatRows(rows).join('\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n'); }\\\\\\\\nexport const publicFixtureSymbols = ['formatInvoice', 'formatRows'];\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"}\\\\n{\\\\\\\"path\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"trace-load/before/facade.mjs\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"import { invoiceAdapter } from './adapter.mjs';\\\\\\\\nexport function invoiceFacade(invoice) { return invoiceAdapter(invoice); }\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"}\\\\n{\\\\\\\"path\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"trace-load/before/formatter.mjs\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"let lastFormatted = '';\\\\\\\\nfunction fixed(value) { return value.toFixed(2); }\\\\\\\\nexport function formatInvoice(value) {\\\\\\\\n  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) throw new Error('nonfinite');\\\\\\\\n  lastFormatted = fixed(value);\\\\\\\\n  return lastFormatted;\\\\\\\\n}\\\\\\\\nexport function formatRows(rows) { return rows.map(row => formatInvoice(row.total)); }\\\\\\\\n// Separate known bug, outside formatter refactor: this sorts numeric strings lexically.\\\\\\\\nexport function sortAmounts(values) { return values.toSorted(); }\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"}\\\\n\\\",\\n  \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\"\\n}\\n```\\n\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"skippedManifest\": [\n    {\n      \"path\": \"fixtures/batch-05/trace-load/before\",\n      \"owner\":       "owner": "local-013-initial-trace-load",
      "kind": "case-response",
      "text": "# Invoice trace refactor\n\nThe working controller now calls calculate(invoice.rows) directly. The facade and adapter copies were removed after caller inspection showed that each had only its predecessor as a caller. Calculation remains a meaningful boundary for the row-sum rule.\n\nInvoice rows have qty and unitPrice fields. sumRows derives total with JavaScript multiplication and ordered addition, starting at 0. It has no stored state. calculate returns that local result and records the last completed result for inspectCachedTotal. The snapshot remains module scoped because the supplied API exposes it. Deleting that state would change consumer behavior.\n\nChanging rows, quantities or prices changes the next calculated result. Mutating rows alone does not update the snapshot. Any completed calculate call, directly or through the controller, replaces the snapshot. Exceptions leave the previous snapshot intact. NaN and Infinity remain accepted by calculation, while the separate display formatter rejects them.\n\n## Grounding and ownership\n\n- Assignment trace-load-native-1, canonical owner /root/trace_load, direct case work. Requested model and effort inherit the parent. Actual model and effort are unknown.\n- Input HEAD was observed before work and after review as 0573bd88232d5adc013159cf592eabc6842e3d11.\n- Installed source root is /Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3.\n- Assigned package freeze is 51f209daf83cd97d64f89881d66533bb59dc57db. The main report revision 84e8ad3efc9368db8da3b192ca139f8718ea9912 is distinct. Neither identifies the fixture HEAD.\n- Supplied task is recorded in task-contract.json and the parent's assignment. It requires tracing Controller through InvoiceFacade and InvoiceAdapter to Calculator, reducing indirection and explaining total changes while preserving meaningful boundaries.\n- Source reads include skills/principle-minimize-reader-load/SKILL.md, skills/poteto-mode/SKILL.md including its Principles index, references/host-capabilities.md, skills/principle-guard-the-context-window/SKILL.md, skills/principle-prove-it-works/SKILL.md, skills/principle-test-behavior-not-implementation/SKILL.md and skills/unslop/SKILL.md. These reads came only from the installed source root. The full mode was read for the Principles index and was not activated as an additional controller.\n- Input reads include README.md, task-contract.json, outputs/assignment.json, committed-inputs/shared/README.md, committed-inputs/shared/invoice.mjs, committed-inputs/shared/additional/README.md, every file in committed-inputs/shared/additional/invoice including the complete decision.md, and caller searches across supplied source modules and documents.\n- No actual invoker of inspectCachedTotal was supplied. Its exported behavior and explicit decision constraint were therefore retained and exercised as a consumer API. No external caller search was claimed.\n\n## Decisions and scope\n\nMinimize Reader Load removed two pass-through layers and made the returned total independent of the snapshot variable. The remaining snapshot's invariant is named once beside its state. Removing it would violate the observable API. This is an explicit compatibility constraint, not a claim that all mutable state disappeared.\n\nProve It Works and Test Behavior, Not Implementation led to executable baseline and after checks of controller results and the inspector, including errors and row mutation. Unslop shaped this report.\n\nFormatter, exporter and their decision file are unchanged. The decision's alternative A avoids an unobserved Formatter instance and unobservable lastFormatted state, while alternative B adds a stateful instance for the reader to track. Neither alternative was implemented because this case targets the total trace. The unrelated sortAmounts defect and the authorized formatter rename remain outside this diff.\n\n## Checks and observed results\n\n- bun outputs/check.mjs outputs/working/invoice ran before and after edits, both exit 0. Raw stdout is identical. It checks ordinary totals, empty rows, negative quantities, fractional values, NaN, Infinity, the initial snapshot, direct calculate updates, exceptions retaining the previous snapshot, and row mutation followed by recalculation.\n- Display baseline and after observations are 0 -> \"0.00\", 1.005 -> \"1.00\", -1.005 -> \"-1.00\", 12.345 -> \"12.35\". NaN and Infinity throw \"nonfinite\". Empty formatRows gives []. CSV output retains the literal backslash-n separator supplied in exporter.mjs.\n- The final review inspected the concrete working diff. Committed input diff was empty, and git status showed only untracked outputs/.\n- outputs/input-hashes.json records SHA-256 for the selected input bytes. Final integrity verification is recorded separately.\n- The first discovery command exited 1 because no matching AGENTS.md was found and its chained HEAD command did not execute. HEAD was obtained in the next command. An overbroad invoice text search returned irrelevant synthetic corpus lines and was truncated. A bounded symbol search then supplied the caller evidence. These are retained as limitations and raw outputs.\n- The under-30-second new-reader test is only a design criterion. No human reader duration was measured. No benchmark or host qualification result is claimed.\n- Review is this owner's source and behavior review. The parent must inspect these artifacts for independent integration review.\n\n## Artifacts and constraints\n\nWorking code is under outputs/working/invoice. The rerunnable check is outputs/check.mjs. Baseline, after, source/caller reads and patch arguments/results are individual outputs/raw/*.raw.json files. outputs/baseline.json and outputs/after.json contain parsed observed outputs. No input bytes, commits, git metadata, external services, schedules or other cases were changed. No installs or subagents were used.\n\nThe source-only early entrypoint reads are visible in the host transcript and the parent's initial-read artifact. Later raw logs retain exact tool arguments/results from this worker. Logging calls themselves are not recursively logged.\n\n## Coordinator artifact inspection\n\nRoot inspected controller, calculator, rerunnable check and both actual JSON observations. The direct path and preserved inspector snapshot match source and baseline/after observations; no reader time was measured. See parent-artifact-inspection.raw.json and parent-report-inspection.raw.json. Input revision still 0573bd88232d5adc013159cf592eabc6842e3d11; final-integrity.raw.json records actual full HEAD, empty input diff and outputs-only status. Worker /root/trace_load returned a terminal final response; /root inspected its artifacts before this note. Execution mode at the batch level is native workers; a worker's description of its own direct implementation does not mean the coordinator executed it sequentially. Actual worker models remain unknown. Initial broad-discovery/read-order and local raw-receipt gaps in the report remain limitations, not repaired history.\n"

      "text": "import assert from 'node:assert/strict';\nimport { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';\nconst root = process.argv[2];\nconst load = file => import(pathToFileURL(`${root}/${file}`).href);\nconst { invoiceController } = await load('controller.mjs');\nconst { calculate, inspectCachedTotal } = await load('calculator.mjs');\nconst { formatInvoice, formatRows } = await load('formatter.mjs');\nconst { exportAmounts } = await load('exporter.mjs');\nconst results = [];\nassert.equal(inspectCachedTotal(), 0);\nfor (const [name, rows, expected] of [\n  ['ordinary', [{ qty: 2, unitPrice: 12.5 }, { qty: 3, unitPrice: 4 }], 37],\n  ['empty', [], 0], ['negative', [{ qty: -2, unitPrice: 3 }], -6],\n  ['fraction', [{ qty: 1, unitPrice: 1.005 }], 1.005],\n  ['nan', [{ qty: NaN, unitPrice: 2 }], NaN],\n  ['infinity', [{ qty: 1, unitPrice: Infinity }], Infinity],\n]) {\n  const actual = invoiceController({ rows });\n  assert.deepEqual(actual, { total: expected });\n  assert.equal(inspectCachedTotal(), expected);\n  results.push({ name, total: String(actual.total), snapshot: String(inspectCachedTotal()) });\n}\nassert.equal(calculate([{ qty: 7, unitPrice: 2 }]), 14);\nassert.throws(() => invoiceController({ rows: null }), TypeError);\nassert.equal(inspectCachedTotal(), 14);\nassert.throws(() => calculate([{ qty: 1, get unitPrice() { throw new Error('row failure'); } }]), /row failure/);\nassert.equal(inspectCachedTotal(), 14);\nconst rows = [{ qty: 2, unitPrice: 5 }];\nassert.deepEqual(invoiceController({ rows }), { total: 10 });\nrows[0].qty = 3;\nassert.equal(inspectCachedTotal(), 10);\nassert.deepEqual(invoiceController({ rows }), { total: 15 });\nassert.equal(inspectCachedTotal(), 15);\nfor (const value of [0, 1.005, -1.005, 12.345, NaN, Infinity]) {\n  let actual;\n  try { actual = formatInvoice(value); } catch (error) { actual = { error: error.message }; }\n  results.push({ formatInput: String(value), actual });\n}\nassert.equal(formatInvoice(0), '0.00');\nassert.equal(formatInvoice(1.005), '1.00');\nassert.equal(formatInvoice(-1.005), '-1.00');\nassert.equal(formatInvoice(12.345), '12.35');\nassert.throws(() => formatInvoice(NaN), { message: 'nonfinite' });\nassert.throws(() => formatInvoice(Infinity), { message: 'nonfinite' });\nassert.deepEqual(formatRows([]), []);\nassert.equal(exportAmounts([{ total: 1.005 }, { total: 12.345 }]), '1.00\\n12.35');\nresults.push({ emptyRows: formatRows([]), export: exportAmounts([{ total: 1.005 }, { total: 12.345 }]), snapshotAfterRowMutationAndRecalculation: inspectCachedTotal() });\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify(results, null, 2));\n"
